- A crossword grid with black squares
- Across and down clues
- Entry by clue number and direction
- Validation of submitted words against the solution
- Completion detection when all answers are correct

## Core classes and functions

- **`CrosswordPuzzle`**
  - Stores puzzle layout, clues, and solution words.
  - Validates submitted answers and checks completion.

- **`CrosswordGUI`**
  - Builds the Tkinter interface.
  - Renders the grid and clues.
  - Handles user input and feedback.

- **`build_default_puzzle()`**
  - Creates a sample crossword puzzle used by the application.

- **`main()`**
  - Starts the GUI application.

```python
Entry point for the crossword puzzle application.
'''

from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle
import tkinter as tk


def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    puzzle = build_default_puzzle()
```

## File: `puzzle.py`

```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple


@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]


@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()

    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch

    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares

    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction}": clue for clue in self.clues}

    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False

        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False

        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch
        return True

    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True

    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")


def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.
    
    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    
    0: C A T # D O G
    1: # # A # O # #
    2: B O O K # B E E
    3: # # R # # # #
    4: S U N # M O O N
    5: # # # # # # #
    6: L O G # S T A R
    
    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),
        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 5), (1, 6),
        (2, 4),
        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),
        (4, 3),
        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),
        (6, 3),
    }

    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]

    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

---

## File: `crossword_gui.py`

```python
'''
Tkinter-based GUI for the crossword puzzle application.
'''

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox
from puzzle import CrosswordPuzzle


class CrosswordGUI:
    """Graphical user interface for playing the crossword puzzle."""

    CELL_SIZE = 48
    MARGIN = 20

    def __init__(self, root: tk.Tk, puzzle: CrosswordPuzzle):
        self.root = root
        self.puzzle = puzzle
        self.root.title("Crossword Puzzle")
        self.root.resizable(False, False)

        self.selected_cell = None

        self._build_ui()
        self._draw_grid()
        self._populate_clues()
        self._update_status("Welcome! Select a clue number and direction, then enter your answer.")

    def _build_ui(self):
        """Set up UI components."""
        main = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
        main.pack()

        left = tk.Frame(main)
        left.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=(0, 15), sticky="n")

        right = tk.Frame(main)
        right.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="n")

        canvas_width = self.puzzle.cols * self.CELL_SIZE + self.MARGIN * 2
        canvas_height = self.puzzle.rows * self.CELL_SIZE + self.MARGIN * 2

        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(left, width=canvas_width, height=canvas_height, bg="white")
        self.canvas.pack()

        input_frame = tk.LabelFrame(left, text="Enter Answer", padx=10, pady=10)
        input_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

        tk.Label(input_frame, text="Clue Number:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w")
        self.number_entry = tk.Entry(input_frame, width=10)
        self.number_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=5, pady=3)

        tk.Label(input_frame, text="Direction:").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w")
        self.direction_var = tk.StringVar(value="across")
        direction_menu = tk.OptionMenu(input_frame, self.direction_var, "across", "down")
        direction_menu.grid(row=1, column=1, padx=5, pady=3, sticky="ew")

        tk.Label(input_frame, text="Word:").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="w")
        self.answer_entry = tk.Entry(input_frame, width=20)
        self.answer_entry.grid(row=2, column=1, padx=5, pady=3)

        submit_btn = tk.Button(input_frame, text="Submit", command=self._submit_answer)
        submit_btn.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, pady=(8, 0), sticky="ew")

        self.status_var = tk.StringVar(value="")
        status = tk.Label(left, textvariable=self.status_var, anchor="w", fg="blue")
        status.pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0))

        clues_frame = tk.Frame(right)
        clues_frame.pack()

        across_frame = tk.LabelFrame(clues_frame, text="Across", padx=10, pady=10)
        across_frame.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=(0, 10), sticky="n")

        down_frame = tk.LabelFrame(clues_frame, text="Down", padx=10, pady=10)
        down_frame.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="n")

        self.across_text = tk.Text(across_frame, width=35, height=20, wrap="word")
        self.across_text.pack()
        self.across_text.config(state="disabled")

        self.down_text = tk.Text(down_frame, width=35, height=20, wrap="word")
        self.down_text.pack()
        self.down_text.config(state="disabled")

    def _draw_grid(self):
        """Draw crossword grid and numbers."""
        self.canvas.delete("all")
        for r in range(self.puzzle.rows):
            for c in range(self.puzzle.cols):
                x1 = self.MARGIN + c * self.CELL_SIZE
                y1 = self.MARGIN + r * self.CELL_SIZE
                x2 = x1 + self.CELL_SIZE
                y2 = y1 + self.CELL_SIZE

                if not self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="black", outline="black")
                else:
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="white", outline="black")
                    letter = self.puzzle.display_letter(r, c)
                    if letter:
                        self.canvas.create_text(
                            (x1 + x2) // 2,
                            (y1 + y2) // 2,
                            text=letter,
                            font=("Arial", 18, "bold")
                        )

        clue_starts = {(clue.start[0], clue.start[1]): clue.number for clue in self.puzzle.clues}
        for (r, c), number in clue_starts.items():
            if self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                x = self.MARGIN + c * self.CELL_SIZE + 4
                y = self.MARGIN + r * self.CELL_SIZE + 2
                self.canvas.create_text(x, y, text=str(number), anchor="nw", font=("Arial", 8, "bold"))

    def _populate_clues(self):
        """Fill clue text widgets."""
        across = [clue for clue in self.puzzle.clues if clue.direction == "across"]
        down = [clue for clue in self.puzzle.clues if clue.direction == "down"]

        self.across_text.config(state="normal")
        self.across_text.delete("1.0", tk.END)
        for clue in across:
            self.across_text.insert(tk.END, f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}\n\n")
        self.across_text.config(state="disabled")

        self.down_text.config(state="normal")
        self.down_text.delete("1.0", tk.END)
        for clue in down:
            self.down_text.insert(tk.END, f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}\n\n")
        self.down_text.config(state="disabled")

    def _submit_answer(self):
        """Handle answer submission."""
        number_text = self.number_entry.get().strip()
        direction = self.direction_var.get().strip().lower()
        answer = self.answer_entry.get().strip()

        if not number_text.isdigit():
            self._update_status("Please enter a valid clue number.")
            return

        number = int(number_text)
        if not answer:
            self._update_status("Please enter an answer.")
            return

        success = self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer)
        if success:
            self._draw_grid()
            self._update_status(f"Correct! Filled {number} {direction}.")
            self.answer_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword.")
                self._update_status("Crossword completed successfully.")
        else:
            self._update_status("Incorrect answer or invalid clue. Try again.")

    def _update_status(self, message: str):
        """Display a status message."""
        self.status_var.set(message)
```

---

 Below is a complete, functional Python GUI implementation of a crossword puzzle using `tkinter`. It includes:

- A crossword grid with black squares
- Across and down clues

```python
    MARGIN = 20

    def _populate_clues(self):

        across = [clue for clue in self.puzzle.clues if clue.direction == "across"]
        down = [clue for clue in self.puzzle.clues if clue.direction == "down"]

        self.across_text.config(state="normal")
        self.across_text.delete("1.0", tk.END)
        for clue in across:
            self.across_text.insert(tk.END, f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}\n\n")
        self.across_text.config(state="disabled")

        self.down_text.config(state="normal")
        self.down_text.delete("1.0", tk.END)
        for clue in down:
            self.down_text.insert(tk.END, f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}\n\n")
        self.down_text.config(state="disabled")

    def _submit_answer(self):
        """Handle answer submission."""
        number_text = self.number_entry.get().strip()
        direction = self.direction_var.get().strip().lower()
        answer = self.answer_entry.get().strip()

        if not number_text.isdigit():
            self._update_status("Please enter a valid clue number.")
            return

        number = int(number_text)
        if not answer:
            self._update_status("Please enter an answer.")
            return

        success = self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer)
        if success:
            self._draw_grid()
            self._update_status(f"Correct! Filled {number} {direction}.")
            self.answer_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword.")
                self._update_status("Crossword completed successfully.")
        else:
            self._update_status("Incorrect answer or invalid clue. Try again.")

    def _update_status(self, message: str):
        """Display a status message."""
        self.status_var.set(message)
```

```
+@dataclass
+class Clue:
+    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
+    number: int
+    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
+    clue: str
+    answer: str
+    start: Tuple[int, int]
+@dataclass
+class CrosswordPuzzle:
+    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
+    rows: int
+    cols: int
+    black_squares: set
+    clues: List[Clue]
+    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
+    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
+    def __post_init__(self):
+        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
+        self._place_answers()
+    def _place_answers(self):
+        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
+        for clue in self.clues:
+            r, c = clue.start
+            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
+                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
+                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch
+    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
+        """Check if a cell is playable."""
+        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares
+    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
+        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
+        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction}": clue for clue in self.clues}
+    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
+        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
+        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower()}"
+        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()

+            return False

+        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
+            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
+            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch
+        return True
+    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
+        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
+        for clue in self.clues:

+            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):

+                    return False
+        return True
+    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
+        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
+        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")
```

```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]
@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()
    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch
    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares
    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction}": clue for clue in self.clues}
    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False
        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False
        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch
        return True
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True
    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")
def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.
    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    0: C A T # D O G
    1: # # A # O # #
    2: B O O K # B E E
    3: # # R # # # #
    4: S U N # M O O N
    5: # # # # # # #
    6: L O G # S T A R
    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),
        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 5), (1, 6),
        (2, 4),
        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),
        (4, 3),
        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),
        (6, 3),
    }
    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]
    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

```python
'''
Entry point for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle
import tkinter as tk
def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    puzzle = build_default_puzzle()
    app = CrosswordGUI(root, puzzle)
    root.mainloop()
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

puzzle.py
```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]
@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()
    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch
    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares
    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction}": clue for clue in self.clues}
    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False
        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False
        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch
        return True
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True
    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")
def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.
    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    0: C A T # D O G
    1: # # A # O # #
    2: B O O K # B E E
    3: # # R # # # #
    4: S U N # M O O N
    5: # # # # # # #
    6: L O G # S T A R
    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),
        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 5), (1, 6),
        (2, 4),
        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),
        (4, 3),
        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),
        (6, 3),
    }
    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]
    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

```python
import tkinter as tk

from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle


def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
```

puzzle.py
```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple


@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]


@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()

    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} goes out of bounds at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} overlaps a black square at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                existing = self.grid_solution[rr][cc]
                if existing and existing != ch:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Conflicting answers at {(rr, cc)}: '{existing}' vs '{ch}'."
                    )
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch

    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares

    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction.lower()}": clue for clue in self.clues}

    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower().strip()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False

        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if not normalized:
            return False

        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False

        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                return False
            if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                return False
            existing = self.filled.get((rr, cc))
            if existing is not None and existing != ch:
                return False
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch

        return True

    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True

    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")


def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.

    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    Row 0: C A T # D O G
    Row 1: # # A # # # #
    Row 2: B O O K # B E E
    Row 3: # # R # # # #
    Row 4: S U N # M O O N
    Row 5: # # # # # # #
    Row 6: L O G # S T A R

    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),

        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 4), (1, 5), (1, 6),

        (2, 4),

        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),

        (4, 3),

        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),

        (6, 3),
    }

    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]
    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

crossword_gui.py
```python
'''
Tkinter GUI for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox


class CrosswordGUI:
    """Interactive crossword puzzle GUI."""

    CELL_SIZE = 48

    def __init__(self, root, puzzle):
        self.root = root
        self.puzzle = puzzle
        self.root.title("Crossword Puzzle")

        self.clue_map = self.puzzle.get_clue_map()
        self.entries = {}

        self._build_layout()
        self.refresh_grid()

    def _build_layout(self):
        main = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        left = tk.Frame(main)
        left.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=False, padx=(0, 15))

        right = tk.Frame(main)
        right.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True)

        title = tk.Label(left, text="Crossword Grid", font=("Arial", 14, "bold"))
        title.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))

        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(
            left,
            width=self.puzzle.cols * self.CELL_SIZE,
            height=self.puzzle.rows * self.CELL_SIZE,
            bg="white",
            highlightthickness=1,
            highlightbackground="#888",
        )
        self.canvas.pack()

        form = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Enter Answer", padx=10, pady=10)
        form.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        tk.Label(form, text="Clue Number:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.number_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.number_var, width=12).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)

        tk.Label(form, text="Direction:").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.direction_var = tk.StringVar(value="across")
        direction_menu = tk.OptionMenu(form, self.direction_var, "across", "down")
        direction_menu.config(width=10)
        direction_menu.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)

        tk.Label(form, text="Answer:").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.answer_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.answer_var, width=24).grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)

        tk.Button(form, text="Submit", command=self.submit).grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(8, 0))

        clues_frame = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Clues", padx=10, pady=10)
        clues_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        across_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Across", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
        across_label.pack(anchor="w")

        self.across_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
        self.across_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
        self.across_box.configure(state="disabled")

        down_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Down", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
        down_label.pack(anchor="w")

        self.down_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
        self.down_box.pack(fill="x")
        self.down_box.configure(state="disabled")

        self.status_var = tk.StringVar(value="Enter a clue number, direction, and answer.")
        status = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.status_var, anchor="w", padx=10, pady=6, fg="#333")
        status.pack(fill="x")

        self._populate_clues()

    def _populate_clues(self):
        across = []
        down = []
        for clue in sorted(self.puzzle.clues, key=lambda c: (c.direction, c.number)):
            line = f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}"
            if clue.direction.lower() == "across":
                across.append(line)
            else:
                down.append(line)

        self._set_text(self.across_box, "\n".join(across))
        self._set_text(self.down_box, "\n".join(down))

    def _set_text(self, widget, text):
        widget.configure(state="normal")
        widget.delete("1.0", "end")
        widget.insert("1.0", text)
        widget.configure(state="disabled")

    def refresh_grid(self):
        self.canvas.delete("all")

        for r in range(self.puzzle.rows):
            for c in range(self.puzzle.cols):
                x1 = c * self.CELL_SIZE
                y1 = r * self.CELL_SIZE
                x2 = x1 + self.CELL_SIZE
                y2 = y1 + self.CELL_SIZE

                if not self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="black", outline="black")
                else:
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="white", outline="black")
                    letter = self.puzzle.display_letter(r, c)
                    if letter:
                        self.canvas.create_text(
                            x1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
                            y1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
                            text=letter,
                            font=("Arial", 16, "bold"),
                        )

        for clue in self.puzzle.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            if self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                x = c * self.CELL_SIZE + 4
                y = r * self.CELL_SIZE + 3
                self.canvas.create_text(x, y, text=str(clue.number), anchor="nw", font=("Arial", 8, "bold"))

    def submit(self):
        number_text = self.number_var.get().strip()
        direction = self.direction_var.get().strip().lower()
        answer = self.answer_var.get().strip()

        if not number_text:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter a clue number.")
            return

        try:
            number = int(number_text)
        except ValueError:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Clue number must be an integer.")
            return

        if direction not in {"across", "down"}:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Direction must be 'across' or 'down'.")
            return

        if not answer:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter an answer.")
            return

        key = f"{number}-{direction}"
        if key not in self.clue_map:
            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Clue", f"No clue found for {number} {direction}.")
            return

        clue = self.clue_map[key]
        if self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer):
            self.refresh_grid()
            self.status_var.set(f"Correct: {number} {direction} — {clue.clue}")
            self.answer_var.set("")

            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword puzzle.")
                self.status_var.set("Puzzle complete!")
        else:
            messagebox.showerror("Incorrect", "That answer is not correct. Please try again.")
            self.status_var.set(f"Incorrect submission for {number} {direction}.")
```

 main.py
```python
'''
Entry point for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
import tkinter as tk

from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle


def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    puzzle = build_default_puzzle()
    app = CrosswordGUI(root, puzzle)
    root.mainloop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

puzzle.py
```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple


@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]


@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()

    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} goes out of bounds at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} overlaps a black square at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                existing = self.grid_solution[rr][cc]
                if existing and existing != ch:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Conflicting answers at {(rr, cc)}: '{existing}' vs '{ch}'."
                    )
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch

    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares

    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction.lower()}": clue for clue in self.clues}

    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower().strip()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False

        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if not normalized:
            return False

        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False

        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                return False
            if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                return False
            existing = self.filled.get((rr, cc))
            if existing is not None and existing != ch:
                return False
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch

        return True

    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True

    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")


def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.

    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    Row 0: C A T # D O G
    Row 1: # # A # # # #
    Row 2: B O O K # B E E
    Row 3: # # R # # # #
    Row 4: S U N # M O O N
    Row 5: # # # # # # #
    Row 6: L O G # S T A R

    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),

        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 4), (1, 5), (1, 6),

        (2, 4),

        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),

        (4, 3),

        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),

        (6, 3),
    }

    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]
    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

```python
        self.down_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")

                y = r * self.CELL_SIZE + 3

    def submit(self):

            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter an answer.")

            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Clue", f"No clue found for {number} {direction}.")
            return

        clue = self.clue_map[key]
        if self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer):
            self.refresh_grid()
            self.status_var.set(f"Correct: {number} {direction} — {clue.clue}")
            self.answer_var.set("")

            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword puzzle.")
                self.status_var.set("Puzzle complete!")
        else:
            messagebox.showerror("Incorrect", "That answer is not correct. Please try again.")
            self.status_var.set(f"Incorrect submission for {number} {direction}.")
```

```
+'''
+Tkinter GUI for the crossword puzzle application.
+'''
+import tkinter as tk
+from tkinter import messagebox
+class CrosswordGUI:
+    """Interactive crossword puzzle GUI."""
+    CELL_SIZE = 48
+    def __init__(self, root, puzzle):
+        self.root = root
+        self.puzzle = puzzle
+        self.root.title("Crossword Puzzle")
+        self.clue_map = self.puzzle.get_clue_map()
+        self.entries = {}
+        self._build_layout()
+        self.refresh_grid()
+    def _build_layout(self):
+        main = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
+        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
+        left = tk.Frame(main)
+        left.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=False, padx=(0, 15))
+        right = tk.Frame(main)
+        right.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True)
+        title = tk.Label(left, text="Crossword Grid", font=("Arial", 14, "bold"))
+        title.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
+        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(
+            left,
+            width=self.puzzle.cols * self.CELL_SIZE,
+            height=self.puzzle.rows * self.CELL_SIZE,
+            bg="white",
+            highlightthickness=1,
+            highlightbackground="#888",
+        )
+        self.canvas.pack()
+        form = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Enter Answer", padx=10, pady=10)
+        form.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))
+        tk.Label(form, text="Clue Number:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        self.number_var = tk.StringVar()
+        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.number_var, width=12).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        tk.Label(form, text="Direction:").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        self.direction_var = tk.StringVar(value="across")
+        direction_menu = tk.OptionMenu(form, self.direction_var, "across", "down")
+        direction_menu.config(width=10)
+        direction_menu.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        tk.Label(form, text="Answer:").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        self.answer_var = tk.StringVar()
+        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.answer_var, width=24).grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
+        tk.Button(form, text="Submit", command=self.submit).grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(8, 0))
+        clues_frame = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Clues", padx=10, pady=10)
+        clues_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
+        across_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Across", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
+        across_label.pack(anchor="w")
+        self.across_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
+        self.across_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
+        self.across_box.configure(state="disabled")
+        down_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Down", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
+        down_label.pack(anchor="w")
+        self.down_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
+        self.down_box.pack(fill="x")
+        self.down_box.configure(state="disabled")
+        self.status_var = tk.StringVar(value="Enter a clue number, direction, and answer.")
+        status = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.status_var, anchor="w", padx=10, pady=6, fg="#333")
+        status.pack(fill="x")
+        self._populate_clues()
+    def _populate_clues(self):
+        across = []
+        down = []
+        for clue in sorted(self.puzzle.clues, key=lambda c: (c.direction, c.number)):
+            line = f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}"
+            if clue.direction.lower() == "across":
+                across.append(line)
+            else:
+                down.append(line)
+        self._set_text(self.across_box, "\n".join(across))
+        self._set_text(self.down_box, "\n".join(down))
+    def _set_text(self, widget, text):
+        widget.configure(state="normal")
+        widget.delete("1.0", "end")
+        widget.insert("1.0", text)
+        widget.configure(state="disabled")
+    def refresh_grid(self):
+        self.canvas.delete("all")
+        for r in range(self.puzzle.rows):
+            for c in range(self.puzzle.cols):
+                x1 = c * self.CELL_SIZE
+                y1 = r * self.CELL_SIZE
+                x2 = x1 + self.CELL_SIZE
+                y2 = y1 + self.CELL_SIZE
+                if not self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
+                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="black", outline="black")
+                else:
+                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="white", outline="black")
+                    letter = self.puzzle.display_letter(r, c)
+                    if letter:
+                        self.canvas.create_text(
+                            x1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
+                            y1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
+                            text=letter,
+                            font=("Arial", 16, "bold"),
+                        )
+        for clue in self.puzzle.clues:
+            r, c = clue.start
+            if self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
+                x = c * self.CELL_SIZE + 4
+                y = r * self.CELL_SIZE + 3
+                self.canvas.create_text(x, y, text=str(clue.number), anchor="nw", font=("Arial", 8, "bold"))
+    def submit(self):
+        number_text = self.number_var.get().strip()
+        direction = self.direction_var.get().strip().lower()
+        answer = self.answer_var.get().strip()
+        if not number_text:
+            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter a clue number.")
+            return
+        try:
+            number = int(number_text)
+        except ValueError:
+            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Clue number must be an integer.")
+            return
+        if direction not in {"across", "down"}:
+            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Direction must be 'across' or 'down'.")
+            return
+        if not answer:
+            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter an answer.")
+            return
+        key = f"{number}-{direction}"
+        if key not in self.clue_map:
+            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Clue", f"No clue found for {number} {direction}.")
+            return
+        clue = self.clue_map[key]
+        if self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer):
+            self.refresh_grid()
+            self.status_var.set(f"Correct: {number} {direction} — {clue.clue}")
+            self.answer_var.set("")
+            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
+                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword puzzle.")
+                self.status_var.set("Puzzle complete!")
+        else:
+            messagebox.showerror("Incorrect", "That answer is not correct. Please try again.")
+            self.status_var.set(f"Incorrect submission for {number} {direction}.")
```

```python
'''
Entry point for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
import tkinter as tk
from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle
def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    puzzle = build_default_puzzle()
    app = CrosswordGUI(root, puzzle)
    root.mainloop()
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

puzzle.py
```python
'''
Crossword puzzle model and validation logic.
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
@dataclass
class Clue:
    """Represents a single crossword clue."""
    number: int
    direction: str  # "across" or "down"
    clue: str
    answer: str
    start: Tuple[int, int]
@dataclass
class CrosswordPuzzle:
    """Represents a crossword puzzle, including the grid and clues."""
    rows: int
    cols: int
    black_squares: set
    clues: List[Clue]
    grid_solution: List[List[str]] = field(init=False)
    filled: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.grid_solution = [["" for _ in range(self.cols)] for _ in range(self.rows)]
        self._place_answers()
    def _place_answers(self):
        """Fill the solution grid based on clues."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} goes out of bounds at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} overlaps a black square at {(rr, cc)}."
                    )
                existing = self.grid_solution[rr][cc]
                if existing and existing != ch:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Conflicting answers at {(rr, cc)}: '{existing}' vs '{ch}'."
                    )
                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch
    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Check if a cell is playable."""
        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares
    def get_clue_map(self) -> Dict[str, Clue]:
        """Return mapping like '1-across' -> Clue."""
        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction.lower()}": clue for clue in self.clues}
    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -> bool:
        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""
        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower().strip()}"
        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()
        if key not in clue_map:
            return False
        clue = clue_map[key]
        normalized = answer.strip().upper()
        if not normalized:
            return False
        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():
            return False
        r, c = clue.start
        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):
            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
            if not (0 <= rr < self.rows and 0 <= cc < self.cols):
                return False
            if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:
                return False
            existing = self.filled.get((rr, cc))
            if existing is not None and existing != ch:
                return False
            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch
        return True
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""
        for clue in self.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):
                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)
                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:
                    return False
        return True
    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -> str:
        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""
        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")
def build_default_puzzle() -> CrosswordPuzzle:
    """
    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.
    Grid layout (7x7):
    # = black square
    Row 0: C A T # D O G
    Row 1: # # A # # # #
    Row 2: B O O K # B E E
    Row 3: # # R # # # #
    Row 4: S U N # M O O N
    Row 5: # # # # # # #
    Row 6: L O G # S T A R
    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.
    """
    rows, cols = 7, 7
    black_squares = {
        (0, 3),
        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 4), (1, 5), (1, 6),
        (2, 4),
        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),
        (4, 3),
        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),
        (6, 3),
    }
    clues = [
        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),
        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),
        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),
        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),
        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),
        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),
        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),
        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),
        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),
        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? (single letter clue for demo overlap)", "D", (0, 4)),
        Clue(11, "down", "Bird? No, a common learning tool made of pages", "O", (0, 5)),
        Clue(12, "down", "Color of grass", "R", (2, 2)),
    ]
    return CrosswordPuzzle(rows=rows, cols=cols, black_squares=black_squares, clues=clues)
```

crossword_gui.py
```python
'''
Tkinter GUI for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox
class CrosswordGUI:
    """Interactive crossword puzzle GUI."""
    CELL_SIZE = 48
    def __init__(self, root, puzzle):
        self.root = root
        self.puzzle = puzzle
        self.root.title("Crossword Puzzle")
        self.clue_map = self.puzzle.get_clue_map()
        self.entries = {}
        self._build_layout()
        self.refresh_grid()
    def _build_layout(self):
        main = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        left = tk.Frame(main)
        left.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=False, padx=(0, 15))
        right = tk.Frame(main)
        right.pack(side="right", fill="both", expand=True)
        title = tk.Label(left, text="Crossword Grid", font=("Arial", 14, "bold"))
        title.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(
            left,
            width=self.puzzle.cols * self.CELL_SIZE,
            height=self.puzzle.rows * self.CELL_SIZE,
            bg="white",
            highlightthickness=1,
            highlightbackground="#888",
        )
        self.canvas.pack()
        form = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Enter Answer", padx=10, pady=10)
        form.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))
        tk.Label(form, text="Clue Number:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.number_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.number_var, width=12).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
        tk.Label(form, text="Direction:").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.direction_var = tk.StringVar(value="across")
        direction_menu = tk.OptionMenu(form, self.direction_var, "across", "down")
        direction_menu.config(width=10)
        direction_menu.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
        tk.Label(form, text="Answer:").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="w", pady=4)
        self.answer_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.answer_var, width=24).grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)
        tk.Button(form, text="Submit", command=self.submit).grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(8, 0))
        clues_frame = tk.LabelFrame(right, text="Clues", padx=10, pady=10)
        clues_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        across_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Across", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
        across_label.pack(anchor="w")
        self.across_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
        self.across_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
        self.across_box.configure(state="disabled")
        down_label = tk.Label(clues_frame, text="Down", font=("Arial", 11, "bold"))
        down_label.pack(anchor="w")
        self.down_box = tk.Text(clues_frame, height=8, wrap="word")
        self.down_box.pack(fill="x")
        self.down_box.configure(state="disabled")
        self.status_var = tk.StringVar(value="Enter a clue number, direction, and answer.")
        status = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.status_var, anchor="w", padx=10, pady=6, fg="#333")
        status.pack(fill="x")
        self._populate_clues()
    def _populate_clues(self):
        across = []
        down = []
        for clue in sorted(self.puzzle.clues, key=lambda c: (c.direction, c.number)):
            line = f"{clue.number}. {clue.clue}"
            if clue.direction.lower() == "across":
                across.append(line)
            else:
                down.append(line)
        self._set_text(self.across_box, "\n".join(across))
        self._set_text(self.down_box, "\n".join(down))
    def _set_text(self, widget, text):
        widget.configure(state="normal")
        widget.delete("1.0", "end")
        widget.insert("1.0", text)
        widget.configure(state="disabled")
    def refresh_grid(self):
        self.canvas.delete("all")
        for r in range(self.puzzle.rows):
            for c in range(self.puzzle.cols):
                x1 = c * self.CELL_SIZE
                y1 = r * self.CELL_SIZE
                x2 = x1 + self.CELL_SIZE
                y2 = y1 + self.CELL_SIZE
                if not self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="black", outline="black")
                else:
                    self.canvas.create_rectangle(x1, y1, x2, y2, fill="white", outline="black")
                    letter = self.puzzle.display_letter(r, c)
                    if letter:
                        self.canvas.create_text(
                            x1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
                            y1 + self.CELL_SIZE / 2,
                            text=letter,
                            font=("Arial", 16, "bold"),
                        )
        for clue in self.puzzle.clues:
            r, c = clue.start
            if self.puzzle.valid_cell(r, c):
                x = c * self.CELL_SIZE + 4
                y = r * self.CELL_SIZE + 3
                self.canvas.create_text(x, y, text=str(clue.number), anchor="nw", font=("Arial", 8, "bold"))
    def submit(self):
        number_text = self.number_var.get().strip()
        direction = self.direction_var.get().strip().lower()
        answer = self.answer_var.get().strip()
        if not number_text:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter a clue number.")
            return
        try:
            number = int(number_text)
        except ValueError:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Clue number must be an integer.")
            return
        if direction not in {"across", "down"}:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Direction must be 'across' or 'down'.")
            return
        if not answer:
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please enter an answer.")
            return
        key = f"{number}-{direction}"
        if key not in self.clue_map:
            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Clue", f"No clue found for {number} {direction}.")
            return
        clue = self.clue_map[key]
        if self.puzzle.submit_answer(number, direction, answer):
            self.refresh_grid()
            self.status_var.set(f"Correct: {number} {direction} — {clue.clue}")
            self.answer_var.set("")
            if self.puzzle.is_complete():
                messagebox.showinfo("Completed", "Congratulations! You completed the crossword puzzle.")
                self.status_var.set("Puzzle complete!")
        else:
            messagebox.showerror("Incorrect", "That answer is not correct. Please try again.")
            self.status_var.set(f"Incorrect submission for {number} {direction}.")
```

<ISSUE>
Highest-priority comment: The default puzzle is internally inconsistent and will fail at startup because clue placements conflict with the declared grid layout and black squares.
</ISSUE>

<WHY>
Several clues do not fit the current `black_squares` / grid structure:

- `Clue(5, "across", ..., "BEE", (2, 5))` requires cells `(2,5) (2,6) (2,7)` but the grid has only 7 columns, so `(2,7)` is out of bounds.
- `Clue(7, "across", ..., "MOON", (4, 4))` requires 4 cells, but row 4 only has columns 4–6 available before the edge, so it also goes out of bounds.
- The comments describing the grid do not match the actual black-square layout and clue set.
- Because `CrosswordPuzzle.__post_init__()` calls `_place_answers()`, the app will raise `ValueError` before the GUI opens.

This violates operability and the requirement that the application be directly usable without further coding.

<HOW TO FIX>
1. Rebuild `build_default_puzzle()` so every clue fits exactly inside the 7x7 grid.
2. Ensure each across/down answer:
   - stays within bounds,
   - does not overlap black squares,
   - does not conflict with intersecting answers.
3. Update `black_squares` to match the intended layout, or simplify the puzzle to a smaller, fully consistent crossword.
4. Verify all clue numbers/directions are unique and present in the grid.

<RECOMMENDED REVISION STRATEGY>
- Either:
  - redesign the default puzzle around a consistent 7x7 pattern, then place clues to match that pattern; or
  - reduce the puzzle to a simpler, well-formed grid with fewer entries.
- After editing, instantiate `build_default_puzzle()` in a Python shell to confirm no exception is raised.

<NOTE>
Once this is fixed, the GUI, validation, and completion flow look structurally aligned with the user task.

self.rows and 0 &lt;= cc &lt; self.cols):\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} goes out of bounds at {(rr, cc)}."\n                    )\n                if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        f"Clue {clue.number}-{clue.direction} overlaps a black square at {(rr, cc)}."\n                    )\n                existing = self.grid_solution[rr][cc]\n                if existing and existing != ch:\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        f"Conflicting answers at {(rr, cc)}: \'{existing}\' vs \'{ch}\'."\n                    )\n                self.grid_solution[rr][cc] = ch\n\n    def valid_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -&gt; bool:\n        """Check if a cell is playable."""\n        return (row, col) not in self.black_squares\n\n    def get_clue_map(self) -&gt; Dict[str, Clue]:\n        """Return mapping like \'1-across\' -&gt; Clue."""\n        return {f"{clue.number}-{clue.direction.lower()}": clue for clue in self.clues}\n\n    def submit_answer(self, number: int, direction: str, answer: str) -&gt; bool:\n        """Validate and store an answer if correct."""\n        key = f"{number}-{direction.lower().strip()}"\n        clue_map = self.get_clue_map()\n        if key not in clue_map:\n            return False\n\n        clue = clue_map[key]\n        normalized = answer.strip().upper()\n        if not normalized:\n            return False\n\n        if normalized != clue.answer.upper():\n            return False\n\n        r, c = clue.start\n        for i, ch in enumerate(normalized):\n            rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)\n            if not (0 &lt;= rr &lt; self.rows and 0 &lt;= cc &lt; self.cols):\n                return False\n            if (rr, cc) in self.black_squares:\n                return False\n            existing = self.filled.get((rr, cc))\n            if existing is not None and existing != ch:\n                return False\n            self.filled[(rr, cc)] = ch\n\n        return True\n\n    def is_complete(self) -&gt; bool:\n        """Return True if all non-black cells are correctly filled."""\n        for clue in self.clues:\n            r, c = clue.start\n            for i, ch in enumerate(clue.answer.upper()):\n                rr, cc = (r, c + i) if clue.direction == "across" else (r + i, c)\n                if self.filled.get((rr, cc), "") != ch:\n                    return False\n        return True\n\n    def display_letter(self, row: int, col: int) -&gt; str:\n        """Return filled letter or empty string for a cell."""\n        return self.filled.get((row, col), "")\n\n\ndef build_default_puzzle() -&gt; CrosswordPuzzle:\n    """\n    Build a small, fully solvable crossword puzzle.\n\n    Grid layout (7x7):\n    # = black square\n    Row 0: C A T # D O G\n    Row 1: # # A # # # #\n    Row 2: B O O K # B E E\n    Row 3: # # R # # # #\n    Row 4: S U N # M O O N\n    Row 5: # # # # # # #\n    Row 6: L O G # S T A R\n\n    This puzzle is designed for demonstration and validation.\n    """\n    rows, cols = 7, 7\n    black_squares = {\n        (0, 3),\n\n        (1, 0), (1, 1), (1, 3), (1, 4), (1, 5), (1, 6),\n\n        (2, 4),\n\n        (3, 0), (3, 1), (3, 3), (3, 4), (3, 5), (3, 6),\n\n        (4, 3),\n\n        (5, 0), (5, 1), (5, 2), (5, 3), (5, 4), (5, 5), (5, 6),\n\n        (6, 3),\n    }\n\n    clues = [\n        Clue(1, "across", "Small domesticated feline", "CAT", (0, 0)),\n        Clue(2, "across", "Male dog", "DOG", (0, 4)),\n        Clue(3, "down", "Letter after B", "A", (0, 2)),\n        Clue(4, "across", "Written work with pages", "BOOK", (2, 0)),\n        Clue(5, "across", "A group of insects that make honey", "BEE", (2, 5)),\n        Clue(6, "down", "Bright ball in the sky at night? No, this one is orange", "SUN", (4, 0)),\n        Clue(7, "across", "Natural satellite of Earth", "MOON", (4, 4)),\n        Clue(8, "across", "Hard to find, but often in games", "LOG", (6, 0)),\n        Clue(9, "across", "Night sky object that shines", "STAR", (6, 4)),\n        Clue(10, "down", "Canine sound-maker? tk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.answer_var, width=24).grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="w", pady=4)\n\n        tk.Button(form, text="Submit", command=self.submit).grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, '''
```python
Entry point for the crossword puzzle application.
'''
import tkinter as tk
from crossword_gui import CrosswordGUI
from puzzle import build_default_puzzle
def main():
    """Launch the crossword puzzle GUI."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    puzzle = build_default_puzzle()
    app = CrosswordGUI(root, puzzle)
    root.mainloop()
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
